Title: The Bee Hive of the Romish Church: a worke of all good Catholikes to be read, and most necessary to be understood; wherein the Catholike Religion is substantially confirmed, and the Heretikes finely fetcht over the coales (the 1623 London printing of Gilpin's English of the Bijenkorf)
Creator: Philips van Marnix van Sint-Aldegonde (the title page's author line is garbled in the OCR; the host catalogue names him); translated into English by George Gilpin the Elder (title page read in the OCR), with Gilpin's dedication to Philip Sidney
Publisher: London: printed by John Dawson (title page read in the OCR as 'John Dam son')
Publication Date: 1623 (title page read in the OCR at file line 42)
Rights: Public Domain (a seventeenth-century English print; public domain by date under the project's 95-year rule)
Source: Internet Archive item bim_early-english-books-1475-1640_the-bee-hive-of-the-romi_marnix-philips-van_1623, file bim_early-english-books-1475-1640_the-bee-hive-of-the-romi_marnix-philips-van_1623_djvu.txt (an EEBO microfilm scan contributed by Internet Archive; collections pub_early-english-books-1475-1640, bim_microfilm), fetched directly (curl, raw bytes) by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, second-pass agent for the continental Reformed and Scandinavian worlds), 2026-10-23; host rights statement: none on the item (no possible-copyright-status field and no licence field; not access-restricted). The other printings on the host (1579 and 1636 sampled by the first pass, 1580 sampled by this pass: black-letter OCR with r-rotunda glyphs, 0.67 of tokens read as words; 1598: the host returned a server error for its text) read worse than this one.
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged apart from long-s to s normalisation (19857 occurrences). Rights basis restated: a seventeenth-century English print; public domain by date under the project's 95-year rule.
Content note: The English beside the vendored Dutch original (marnix_bijenkorf-der-h-roomsche-kerke-nld_1858.txt, the 1569 text in an 1858 edition). Gilpin's translation was first printed in 1579; this is the 1623 reprint, chosen for its OCR. Contents as the OCR shows them: title page (file lines 1-42), Gilpin's dedication to Master Philip Sidney (from line 49), then Marnix's satire in its books and chapters ('The fifth Chapter' at line 12943, 'The fourth Chapter' at 31913 are the headings the OCR catches; the rest are lost in the black letter). The author line of the title page did not survive the OCR; the attribution rests on the host catalogue and on the work itself, which is Gilpin's rendering of the Bijenkorf. Black-letter microfilm OCR: 19,857 long-s glyphs normalised to s; OCR sampled at 10/30/50/70/90 percent (about 0.73 of tokens read as words after normalisation, against a clean English baseline of 0.92), with frequent letter substitutions in the black letter ('bzing' for 'bring', 'og' for 'or'), lost lines and marginal noise. SECOND WITNESS for exact wording (INTAKE scan-quality rule): locate a passage here, verify against the page image, and treat the Dutch 1858 text as the primary wording.
